<!-- ABOUTME: Reports fixed-inventory suite debt, current list evidence and continuing reporting custody without inferring repair from execution. -->
# Existing-suite health

The latest measured sample is Q1 `db4c55c1ff52faac6e40acae08951359661d7f9e`, collected after the main integrations described in “Q1 refresh” below. That section owns the latest memberships and quality-evidence status. The complete earlier Q text, tables, overlay and receipt scopes are preserved below as a historical sample; its statements about pending work describe that earlier sample. Neither a passing run nor unchanged test text transfers a historical quality judgment to Q1.

First source sample uses measurement code Q `62ddc3ec4a7a41fcc444a4f61380803fb9a10c7e`, tree `b40fcc4e4d838bd075a833d852d2f706a401e843`, measured on 2026-11-07. Fresh independent list collection and reported-count reproduction are now recorded for the uncommitted document snapshots identified below. Most current S-to-Q applicability, the later genuine refresh and final-head verification remain incomplete; #1133 stays open. Later receipt-only document edits are not new test/source execution. No flake rate is estimated.

## Population and evidence scopes

The immutable [reviewed inventory](spikes/existing-suite-audit.md) owns the S identities and original scoped judgments. Its [JSON ledger](spikes/existing-suite-audit.json) is 99,820,729 bytes, SHA-256 `fe98d55e74975c3d8ddfd9b223b48f070079d11baba44cf6003ea66f68e675b1`, Git blob `a2610afc9029feac4bda88cb3a2a3ad9631c0c36`. This report changes none of those bytes.

| Sample | Ref | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| S | `2e2640702a328b2e4dd705bd0858aeb414002a6b` | Frozen source audit; S was not executed. |
| M | `fe4782a370e96ebc3bfbadb8b1bd468cb79bd5b1` | Historical native receipts retained by the inventory. |
| Qprep | `93a24132c9272670473ed576aa5efd00f8759e20` | Historical source preparation; no fresh execution sample claimed. |
| Q | `62ddc3ec4a7a41fcc444a4f61380803fb9a10c7e` | Actual deterministic baseline and three list invocations; current applicability coverage below. |

The unique S denominator is **6,368 source domains**: 6,115 ordinary identities (3,912 deterministic and 2,203 host at S) plus 253 explicit generated-source member identities. The generated members expand 132 family rows and include eight suite members. They remain a separate fixture scope; neither family rows nor constructor/invocation contexts are extra ordinary tests. The ledger has 6,247 representation rows, which is not this denominator. Each identity has five axis cells, giving 31,840 cells; multi-axis findings never increase the population.

M reports 4,227 deterministic outcomes (4,127 named cases and 100 file-load nodes) and 2,201 host outcomes. Q's operand-free deterministic run reports 5,108 tests, 5,099 pass, zero fail/cancelled/todo and nine source-qualified case-folding skips. These runtime populations differ from S. No full Q host run, native macOS result, gated provider result or generated child verdict is supplied by Q's deterministic baseline. Complete originals and measurement custody are in the [journal](journal/2026-11-07-0447-1133-suite-health.md).

## Frozen S audit baseline

The following measurements count the inventory's expanded ordinary `axisClassifications` and explicit generated members' `axes[axis].classification`, independently of its aggregate tables. "Retained" preserves the audit's bounded claim; it is not execution proof. "Defective" is a diagnosed test-quality cell. Repaired means an originally defective S cell with accepted current repair evidence; none is credited here.

Ordinary S evidence follows exact source-specific and member applicability: `sourceSpecificAxes`, `sourceAxes` and finite `sourceSpecific` selections take their actual S scope; exact `memberAxisOverrides` supersede shared defaults for the selected member only. Local `$ref` JSON pointers must be resolved before interpreting evidence. The 19 source-only gated identities use their own `gatedDomainAudit`, including registration predicates and unavailable execution. Generated members use their explicit identities, source variant, parent and constructor/phase scope. Sibling titles, family summaries and parent passes supply no missing member evidence. The expanded identity rows above preserve that reviewed historical result; this report does not redo or widen the audit's judgments.

## Q current-evidence overlay

Current classification revision: 2026-11-07, following coordinator source adoption at 09:44:52Z, at unchanged code Q. The overlay now has 145 explicit records: the preserved twenty cells plus 125 records for 25 direct package-budget identities. Of those new records, 123 are retained-source and two discrimination cells remain explicitly unresolved. Overall, 143 cells are retained-source and 31,697 unresolved, including 31,695 default-unresolved cells and the two explicit holes. Placement is known deterministic for all 29 ordinary identities represented by the overlay; 27 have all five axes retained and two have four retained axes plus one unresolved axis. Normal test entry remains a premise; no actual worker argv, module-init, CLI guard, native filesystem/cleanup, mutation, repair, retirement or conversion success follows. Original S tiers remain comparison strata.

| Q evidence grouped by original S scope | Axis scope | Identities | Retained cells, each axis | Defective | Unresolved cells, each axis | Repaired |
|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| S deterministic stratum | expectation, timingLoad, implementationCoupling, fakeHelperContract | 3912 | 29 | 0 | 3883 | 0 |
| S deterministic stratum | discrimination | 3912 | 27 | 0 | 3885 | 0 |
| S host stratum | each of five axes | 2203 | 0 | 0 | 2203 | 0 |
| Generated fixture stratum | each of five axes | 253 | 0 | 0 | 253 | 0 |

By actual current tier, 29 ordinary identities are known deterministic and 6,086 ordinary identities have **unresolved placement**; 253 generated identities retain their separate fixture scope. For expectation, timingLoad, implementationCoupling and fakeHelperContract, known deterministic placement has 29 retained cells per axis. For discrimination it has 27 retained and two explicitly unresolved cells. Unknown ordinary placement contributes 6,086 unresolved cells per axis; generated scope contributes 253. Across all five axes there are 143 retained-source and 31,697 unresolved cells out of 31,840. Known tier placement is not complete source-quality settlement: 27 identities have five retained axes and two have four of five. No defective-new, repaired or retired S obligation credit is added. The 6,368-identity denominator is unchanged.

The finite overlay below selects the immutable ordinary and explicit generated identity sets. Its unresolved default applies separately to every identity/axis key except the 145 explicit records; two of those records deliberately remain unresolved with known deterministic placement. Each cell names its identity, axis, current tier, status, owner, Q source ref and evidence record. The shared idrows-static-source record pins the exact cohort and independent ruling; its scope applies only to those three identities. The separate package-budget-one-case-static-source record binds only the newly adopted identity and five explicit per-axis rationales/limits. The package-budget-direct-static-source record separately binds the 125 newly admitted records and their per-axis rationales/limits, including two unresolved discrimination cells. These cohorts have disjoint identity/axis keys and are counted once each. Duplicate/unknown keys are refused. A repaired entry needs the diagnosed S cell and actual repair proof; a retirement needs owner authority and conserved-obligation evidence. Missing evidence stays unresolved. No blanket inherited-current-retained rule is used.

## Repeatable collection

Use an isolated checkout at the declared sample ref and freeze its tree, unchanged suite blob and immutable ledger digest. Obtain normal execution admission and retain direct argv/cwd/ref, start/PID, complete separate raw streams, direct exit, EOF/recorder and positively observed PID/start-identity closure. Reuse the existing physical slot and capture; do not run Rigger from the checkout. Record the actual results, including every failed attempt. Do not rerun the deterministic baseline merely to obtain counts.

The list authority is literal and exits before the runner's Node startup:

```sh
sh test/suite.sh --list converting
sh test/suite.sh --list awaiting
sh test/suite.sh --list host
```

Keep each raw membership and direct exit before sorting; refuse duplicates or paths absent at the same ref. Derive counts from those outputs. Obtain historical and Q axis counts from the immutable JSON plus the overlay, using the inline data procedure below. It reads data only and installs no utility. It prints canonical historical partitions, current partitions by original S stratum and current-tier coverage. Evidence interpretation must retain the scoped precedence above; the procedure supplies no new semantic or execution proof.

```sh
python3 - <<'PY'
import collections, hashlib, json, pathlib
raw = pathlib.Path('docs/spikes/existing-suite-audit.json').read_bytes()
report = pathlib.Path('docs/testing-suite-health.md').read_text()
data = json.loads(raw)
text = report.split('<!-- sample-overlay:start -->', 1)[1]
overlay = json.loads(text.split('```json\n', 1)[1].split('\n```', 1)[0])
if hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest() != overlay['ledgerSHA256']:
    raise ValueError('immutable ledger differs')
axes = overlay['axes']
ordinary = [x for x in data['sourceDeclarations']
            if not (isinstance(x.get('domain'), dict) and 'memberIds' in x['domain'])]
generated = data['generatedSourceAudit']['members']
items = [(x['sourceId'], x['tier'], x['axisClassifications']) for x in ordinary]
items += [(x['memberId'], 'generated-fixture',
           {a: x['axes'][a]['classification'] for a in axes}) for x in generated]
if len(items) != len({x[0] for x in items}):
    raise ValueError('duplicate identity')
known = {x[0] for x in items}
changes = {}
for cell in overlay['cells']:
    key = cell['identity'], cell['axis']
    if key in changes or key[0] not in known or key[1] not in axes:
        raise ValueError('duplicate or unknown overlay cell')
    if cell['status'] not in {'retained', 'unresolved', 'defective', 'repaired'}:
        raise ValueError('unknown current status')
    if cell['currentTier'] not in {'deterministic', 'host', 'unresolved'}:
        raise ValueError('unknown current tier')
    changes[key] = cell
identity_tiers = {}
for identity, scope, _ in items:
    tiers = {changes[identity, a]['currentTier'] for a in axes
             if (identity, a) in changes and
             changes[identity, a]['currentTier'] != 'unresolved'}
    if len(tiers) > 1:
        raise ValueError('conflicting identity placement')
    identity_tiers[identity] = ('generated-fixture' if scope == 'generated-fixture'
                                else next(iter(tiers), 'unresolved'))
historical = collections.Counter()
current = collections.Counter()
placement = collections.Counter()
for identity, scope, classifications in items:
    for axis in axes:
        historical[scope, axis, classifications[axis]] += 1
        cell = changes.get((identity, axis), overlay['defaultCell'])
        current[scope, axis, cell['status']] += 1
        tier = identity_tiers[identity]
        placement[tier, axis, cell['status']] += 1
for label, values in [('S', historical), ('Q-by-S-scope', current),
                      ('Q-current-tier', placement)]:
    for key, count in sorted(values.items()):
        print(label, *key, count)
print('unique-S', len(items), 'ordinary', len(ordinary), 'generated', len(generated))
ordinary_ids = {x['sourceId'] for x in ordinary}
identity_placement = collections.Counter(identity_tiers[x] for x in ordinary_ids)
quality = collections.Counter()
for identity in ordinary_ids:
    statuses = [changes.get((identity, a), overlay['defaultCell'])['status'] for a in axes]
    retained_axes = statuses.count('retained')
    if retained_axes == len(axes):
        quality['complete-five-axis-retained'] += 1
    elif retained_axes:
        quality['partial-retained'] += 1
    else:
        quality['no-retained-axis'] += 1
explicit_statuses = collections.Counter(c['status'] for c in changes.values())
print('explicit-records', len(changes), 'explicit-statuses', dict(explicit_statuses))
print('default-unresolved-cells', len(items) * len(axes) - len(changes))
print('ordinary-placement', dict(identity_placement))
print('ordinary-quality', dict(quality))
PY
```

For each later sample, an independent collector must obtain the same full ledger, named source and fresh literal list outputs, apply the overlay, and derive every membership/count without using maker totals as an oracle. ### Consumed-source and native-proof correspondence

The independent 230-key qualification resolves 225 bounded-source mappings, four D-2909 comment-only mappings and H-1617's authorized retarget. Its rationale groups name their exact member keys. They account for changed shared helpers, contracts, instructions, imports, registration and tier controls; unchanged bytes or a previous pass alone does not establish applicability.

The shared runner changes add the `saxes` dependency and timer-refusal capture/enforcement. They preserve the selected host, awaiting and conversion membership. The bounded oracles remain independently authored; no timer or producer output becomes an expected value. The per-key record preserves each original finite rationale and its limits through its terminal evidence reference.

H-1617's new ordinary control distinguishes qualified status 1/empty from unknown 0/2/3/255/null observations. It preserves raw-field immutability and the pgrep-only command log. The explicit #1292 grant owns this stronger current control and its move. The old parsed-empty negative characterization remains correct at C; this view adds no historical bug or unrelated-axis repair claim.

The original H-2024 and D-2846 repaired endpoints stay fixed at R. `test/process-fixtures.mjs`, `test/fixture-lifetime.test.mjs` and `test/suite-tiers.test.mjs` preserve their R leaves at the delivered source. `test/once.test.mjs` retains the complete 113,324-byte R prefix, then appends optional-config imports/tests. The independent qualification accounts for those imports and their lazy fixture allocation; whole-file equality is not claimed. The later native witness has its own changed proof source in `test/process-fixtures.test.mjs`.

Actual [run 42685497089](https://github.com/williacj/rigger/actions/runs/42685497089) executed candidate `383d248e21fa84796329b1159ac5f3e2f88bb242`, tree `36d6435dd3d3364c8a0062e1aa3a2643723b3f7d`, on darwin ARM64/Node 26.13.0. The delivered028a tree is that exact tree. The ordinary worker reports 5,545 tests/pass and zero failures, cancellations or skips. The host invocation's recorded native PID 1368 exits 0 with null signal, complete capture and no I/O failures. Its Node summary reports 2,779 tests, 2,751 pass and 28 skips, with zero failures/cancellations. Its JUnit census has 2,776 leaves and the same 28 skips; these are distinct populations. No 76-tree execution is claimed.

The five relevant native witnesses are unique, unskipped and nonfailed in that JUnit evidence. The actual PS absence observation is qualified against an independently reaped child. Actual pgrep observes an owned live child and then qualified no-match after close. Failed spawn records error/close without an invented exit, kill or unhandled rejection. The repaired abnormal caller records late-owned-fixture publication, registered CLI exit, final owner cleanup, actual close and deferred removal. Original controlled delayed-close, guarded fault/restoration and prelaunch readiness proofs remain separately required and retain their original source scopes.

Native zombie-positive behavior remains unobserved. All 28 host skips remain unproved branches. The final owner is `sweep(agent.dir)` after direct CLI exit; this is no arbitrary-descendant or inherited-pipe guarantee. Historical failed 42660079149 retains its unknown cause. New witness success supplies neither an R combined-tree execution nor a retrospective repair of R's old witness. The other 228 cohort judgments retain source-only limits and receive no new runtime credit from the two-key qualification.

### Parent obligations and delivery boundary

Parent #1134 and #739 remain open. This view does not settle the other axes of represented identities, the 5,999 ordinary identities without current placement, generated-fixture evidence, full conversion/awaiting-split work, or a later genuine #1133 measurement refresh. H-2024/discrimination and the shared running/sweep repair remain separate parent coverage; their delivered repair alone adds no adopted cell to this named view.

External ownership remains reserved: #1169, paused #606, #1119, #1129, #1264?#1266, #1271, #1275 and #1305?#1317. #1308 absorbs the old #1128 work; this record resumes no old campaign. The H12 public-comment prohibition remains in force. Unknown or stale external evidence is left uncredited rather than silently accepted or declared absent.

The continuing coordinator retains reporting/publication accountability under the existing #1133 acknowledgment. This repository record prepares the governed health/journal delivery. The coordinator owns the parent index and public provenance summary; independent whole-card judgments and normal forge gates still govern delivery. No new recorder, custody protocol, threshold, policy or gate is introduced.
